Given p_0>0, the coefficients c_k of P^2 determine p_k recursively:
c_0 = p_0^2.
c_k = 2 p_0 p_k + ∑_{j=1}^{k-1} p_j p_{k-j} for k≥1.

Let's define recurrence more generally:
Given target left coefficients l_k for k=0..n-1 (nonnegative), with l_0>0. Set p_0=sqrt(l_0). For k=1..n-1:
    p_k = (l_k - sum_{j=1}^{k-1} p_j p_{k-j}) / (2 p_0)
where sum is over previous p only (j=1..k-1); but coefficient l_k includes p0 p_k + p_k p0 + previous cross terms. Derivation: c_k = 2p0 p_k + ∑_{j=1}^{k-1} 2? Wait c_k = ∑_{j=0}^k p_j p_{k-j}. Split j=0: p0 p_k; j=k: p_k p0; if k=0 handled; for j=1..k-1: p_j p_{k-j} each ordered? The sum over j includes both j and k-j separately if distinct, so ∑_{j=1}^{k-1} p_j p_{k-j} already counts ordered pairs? For k=3, j=1: p1p2, j=2:p2p1; yes sum counts both. So c_k = 2p0 p_k + ∑_{j=1}^{k-1} p_j p_{k-j}. Good. Thus p_k=(l_k - convprev_k)/(2p0), where convprev=∑_{j=1}^{k-1} p_j p_{k-j}.
No factor 2 in sum.
For l_k constant L, p0=√L, shape.

Lowdim modes with Chebyshev:
```
def _make_modes(n, n_cheb, periodic_f=0, include_log=True, rng maybe):
    idx = np.arange(n, dtype=float)
    x = (idx+0.5)/n
    eps = 1.0/(2*n)
    modes=[]
    if include_log:
       T=np.log(x+eps); T-=T.mean(); modes.append(T)
       T=np.log(1.0-x+eps); T-=T.mean(); modes.append(T)
    T=x-x.mean(); modes.append(T)
    T=(x*x)-(x*x).mean(); modes.append
    # maybe (1-x)^2 centered
    # Chebyshev T_k(2x-1) for k=1..K, centered
    y=2*x-1
    c_prev=1? T_0=1; T_1=y
    for k=1..K:
      if k==1: Tn=y
      elif k==2: Tn=2y^2-1
      else: Tn=2*y*Tn_prev - Tn_prevprev
      Tn -= Tn.mean(); modes.append
    # sin Chebyshev? Strategies? Could add S_k=sin(kθ) for k=1..K/2 using recurrence. But maybe not.
    # periodic high f: for f=1..Fp: T=cos(2π f x); T-=mean; sin similarly.
```

3. `_cross_corr(p,w)`: as previous. Need w length 2n-1. If using full, w sum? We need output length n.
```
def _cross_corr(p,w):
 p=np.asarray(p,float); n=p.size; w=np.asarray(w,float)
 M=n+len(w)-1; N=1<<(M-1).bit_length()
 A=np.fft.rfft(p[::-1],N); W=np.fft.rfft(w,N)
 full=np.fft.irfft(A*W,N)[:M]
 return full[n-1:2*n-1]
```

4. `_lse_general(a, beta, topk=0)`:
```
arr=np.asarray(a,float); n=arr.size; if n==0 inf zero
if any not finite: replace 0
S=sum(arr)
if S<1e-12: return 1e10, zeros
c=_self_conv(arr)
# c may negative small: c=np.maximum(c,0)
d = float(n)*c/(S*S)
M=len(c)
if topk<=0 or topk>=M:
   dmax=d.max(); if dmax<=0: return ..., zeros
   z=beta*(d-dmax); e=np.exp(z); se=e.sum()
   w=e/se
   L=dmax+np.log(se)/beta
   corr=_cross_corr(arr,w)
   ldc=float(w@d)
   val=2*L
   grad=4*n*corr/(S*S) -4*ldc/S
   return val, grad
else:
   idx=np.argpartition(d, M-topk)[M-topk:]
   d_act=d[idx]; dmax=d_act.max()
   z=beta*(d_act-dmax); e=...; w_act=e/se; L=...
   w=np.zeros(M); w[idx]=w_act
   corr=...
   ldc=w_act@d_act
   val=2*L
   grad=...
```
Could use full active for lowdim normalized to avoid topk? Maybe topk=0 full.
Numerical: `se` can be up to min(k,M); if beta small, all weights. `np.log(se)/beta` safe. If beta huge and dmax maybe 0.
If `dmax` <0 due negative? c clipped.

But p parameterization p raw/sum; if we use general gradient (including S term) and then chain with `_lowdim_fun` transformation, is it correct? p is normalized, S=1. The general gradient includes derivative of S for arbitrary a direction; but our variation d a from u has first-order change in S=0 because p remains normalized (∑∂p_i=0). The S term derivative along tangent is ∂F_S * dS = ∂F_S *0. However general gradient dot dp may include a term from S that should be zero if dp tangent. The formula `grad_general · dp` with dp tangent should equal normalized gradient dot dp because the component of gradient normal to sum is removed? def _lowdim_fun(x, base, modes, beta, topk):
    # x 1d params
    v = modes @ x  # if modes shape (R,n), x (R,), result (n)
    # center? Could subtract max for stability
    logb = np.log(base) maybe precompute in args? To avoid recompute, pass logbase in args.
    z = logbase + v
    zmax = z.max()
    if zmax > 50: z -= zmax? Actually exp(z-zmax) safe; but then p scale arbitrary; we can do z -= np.median(z) maybe.
    e = np.exp(z - zmax)
    s = e.sum()
    if s<=0: return 1e10, zeros
    p = e/s
    val, gp = _lse_general(p,beta,topk)
    ao = float(gp @ p)
    gparams = modes @ (p*(gp-ao))
    # Since modes may not be centered, this is exact tangent.
    return val, gparams
```

       res = minimize(_direct_fun, a, jac=True, method='L-BFGS-B', bounds=[(0.0,None)]*n, args=(beta,1e-3,topk,0.0), options=opts)

But `_direct_fun` needs penalty maybe optional. Define:
```
def _direct_fun(x, beta, pen, topk):
    val, grad = _lse_general(x,beta,topk)
    S=x.sum()
    if pen>0:
      val += pen*(S-1.0)**2
      grad = grad + 2*pen*(S-1.0)
    return val, grad
```

9. Subgrad polish implementation:
```
def _subgrad_polish(a, max_iters=20, deadline=None, topk=500):
   best_a=a; best_sc=score
   for it in range(max_iters):
      if deadline... break
      c=_self_conv(a); S=sum; d=n*c/S*S
      dmax=d.max()
      M=len(d); K=min(topk, max(10,M))
      idx=np.argpartition(d, M-K)[M-K:] # top K
      # maybe keep only near max: vals=d[idx]; if max(vals)>0, keep vals >= dmax - max(1e-8,1e-6*dmax)
      active within; if none top1; if >K topK
      w zeros; w[active]=1/len
      corr=_cross_corr(a,w); wc=float(w[d active?]) # could use dmax if all equal? use max
      grad=4*n*corr/(S*S) -4*n*dmax/(S*S*S)? Since wc=dmax.
      # more generally wc = w@d
      grad=...
      gnorm=linalg.norm(grad)
      if small break
      trial_step: scales=(0.001,0.003,0.01,0.03,0.1,0.3,1.0)
      for scale: eta=scale/gnorm; q=a - eta*grad; q=np.clip(q,0,None); Sq=sum; if small continue; q/=Sq; sc=score(q); if sc<best_trial:
      if best_trial<best_sc: update else break
   return best
```
This uses topK active weights not exact subgradient if all topK not at max, but approximate. For exact subgradient, set active only at max (or near) so wc≈dmax. We can set K=50 and tol. Good.

Let's outline final code:
```
import numpy as np, time, scipy.optimize
# helpers...

def central_base(n):
    r=np.empty(n); r[0]=1.0
    for i in range(1,n): r[i]=r[i-1]*(2*i-1)/(2*i)
    return r
```
For n=4096 loop okay. Could vectorize using product but loop fine.

def _run_direct(a0, beta, time_budget, maxfun_cap=1500):
  a=np.asarray(a0,float).copy(); n=a.size
  if time_budget<3: return score(a), a
  est=0.008*max(1.0,n/4096.0)
  maxfun=max(30,min(maxfun_cap,int(0.8*time_budget/est)))
  def no closure; use args

```

def _polish_sequence(a0, budget_s, stop_deadline, label=''):
  start=time.monotonic(); end=min(stop_deadline,start+budget_s)
  a0=_norm(a0); best_a=a0.copy(); best_sc=_score(a_cur)
  stages=[(10,0.12),(50,0.14),(200,0.18),(1000,0.2),(5000,0.18),(20000,0.12),(80000,0.06)]
  # Maybe if n>10000 reduce high beta? okay.
  cur=a_cur.copy()
  for beta,frac in stages:
    rem=end-time.monotonic()
    if rem<10: break
    b=max(5, rem*frac)
    sc, out = _run_direct(cur, beta, b, 1500)
    if np.isfinite(sc):
       if sc < best_sc - 1e-12:
          best_sc=sc; best_a=out.copy(); print
       # keep cur=out if finite else cur
       cur=out.copy()
  if end - time.monotonic() >30:
    sc2,a2=_subgrad_polish(best_a, end - time.monotonic() - 5, stop_deadline-5, label)
    if sc2<best_sc: update
  return best_sc,best_a
```

```

  if time > stop_deadline-2 break
  a=_norm(a); c=_self_conv(a); S=s; d=n*c/S*S; dmax=d.max()
  M=len(d); K=min(500,max(20,M//50?))
  idx=np.argpartition(d,M-K)[M-K:]
  vals=d[idx]
  tol=max(1e-12,1e-7*dmax)
  act = idx[vals >= dmax - tol]
  if len(act)==0: act=idx[:1]
  w=np.zeros(M); w[act]=1/len(act)
  corr=_cross_corr(a,w)
  wc=float(w@d)  # d not c? If using d, wc_d. grad general? Let's use c: wc_c=float(w@c)
  grad=4*n*corr/(S*S) -4*n*wc_c/(S*S*S)
  gnorm=norm; if <1e-14 break
  best_step=None; best_sc_iter=best_sc
  for scale in (0.002,0.008,0.02,0.06,0.15,0.4):
    eta=scale/gnorm; q=a-eta*grad; q=clip; Sq=sum; if Sq<1e-8 continue; q/=Sq; sc=_score(q); if sc<best_sc_iter: ...
  if best_step is not None and best_sc_iter<best_sc: update, a=best_step else break

```python
"""
Search for non-negative coefficients minimizing:
    2*n*max(np.convolve(a,a))/sum(a)**2

This program combines several new ideas:
1. A critical "central binomial" base P(x) ~ (1-x)^(-1/2), which is a strong global
   prior for the nonnegative polynomial-square problem.
2. A low-dimensional log-manifold optimizer:
       p_i = base_i * exp(sum_r theta_r T_r(i/n)) / sum
   where T_r are Chebyshev/log/polynomial modes.  This searches smooth but richly
   shaped global candidates quickly, without the scale direction being active.
3. Full-coefficient LSE-smoothed L-BFGS-B polishing on the original nonnegative
   simplex (with sum penalty), followed by exact subgradient polishing.
4. Multiscale length continuation and projection of the current best sequence back
   onto the low-dimensional manifold to refine improved basins.

No filesystem/network IO is used.  The final sequence is returned by
propose_candidate().
"""

import time
import numpy as np
from scipy.optimize import minimize


def _next_pow2(m):
    m = int(max(1, m))
    return 1 << (m - 1).bit_length()


def _self_conv(a):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    n = a.size
    if n == 0:
        return np.empty(0, dtype=np.float64)
    M = 2 * n - 1
    N = _next_pow2(M)
    A = np.fft.rfft(a, N)
    c = np.fft.irfft(A * A, N)[:M]
    c = np.nan_to_num(c, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)
    # Convolution of nonnegative data should be nonnegative; remove roundoff.
    c[c < 0.0] = 0.0
    return c


def _cross_corr(p, w):
    """
    v[i] = sum_k w[k] * p[k-i], valid indices, for i=0..len(p)-1.
    This is the adjoint needed for derivatives of the linear convolution p*p.
    """
    p = np.asarray(p, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    w = np.asarray(w, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    n = p.size
    if n == 0 or w.size == 0:
        return np.empty(n, dtype=np.float64)
    M = n + w.size - 1
    N = _next_pow2(M)
    A = np.fft.rfft(p[::-1], N)
    W = np.fft.rfft(w, N)
    full = np.fft.irfft(A * W, N)[:M]
    out = full[n - 1:2 * n - 1]
    out = np.nan_to_num(out, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)
    return out


def _norm(a):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    if a.size == 0:
        return a
    a = np.nan_to_num(a, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)
    a = np.maximum(a, 0.0)
    s = float(a.sum())
    if s <= 1e-300:
        return np.full(a.size, 1.0 / a.size, dtype=np.float64)
    return a / s


def _score(a):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    n = a.size
    if n == 0:
        return np.inf
    s = float(a.sum())
    if s < 1e-12:
        return np.inf
    c = _self_conv(a)
    if c.size == 0:
        return np.inf
    cm = float(c.max())
    if not np.isfinite(cm):
        return np.inf
    return float(2.0 * n * cm / (s * s))


def _upsample(a, n):
    a = _norm(a)
    m = a.size
    if m == 0:
        return np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64) / n
    if m == n:
        return a.copy()
    if m == 1:
        return np.full(n, a[0] / n, dtype=np.float64)
    x0 = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, m)
    x1 = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n)
    out = np.interp(x1, x0, a)
    return _norm(out)


def _central_base(n):
    """
    Unnormalized critical base:
        b_i = binomial(2i,i) / 4^i,  i=0..n-1
    i.e. the first n coefficients of (1-z)^(-1/2).
    """
    b = np.empty(n, dtype=np.float64)
    b[0] = 1.0
    for i in range(1, n):
        b[i] = b[i - 1] * (2.0 * i - 1.0) / (2.0 * i)
    b = np.nan_to_num(b, nan=1.0 / n, posinf=1.0 / n, neginf=1.0 / n)
    b = np.maximum(b, 1e-300)
    return b


def _remaining(deadline, reserve=0.0):
    r = deadline - time.monotonic() - float(reserve)
    return max(0.0, r)


def _lse_general(a, beta, use_topk=0):
    """
    LSE-smoothed objective and gradient for
        F(a) ~= 2 * LSE_beta( n * conv(a,a) / sum(a)^2 )
    use_topk=0 means all convolution positions.
    """
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    n = a.size
    if n == 0:
        return 1e10, np.empty(0, dtype=np.float64)
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(a)):
        a = np.nan_to_num(a, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)
    s = float(a.sum())
    if s <= 1e-12:
        return 1e10, np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)

    c = _self_conv(a)
    M = c.size
    d = (float(n) * c) / (s * s)

    if M == 0:
        return 1e10, np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)

    if use_topk > 0 and use_topk < M:
        k = int(max(1, min(M, use_topk)))
        idx = np.argpartition(d, M - k)[M - k:]
        d_act = d[idx]
        dmax = float(d_act.max())
        if dmax <= 0.0 or not np.isfinite(dmax):
            return 1e10, np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
        z = beta * (d_act - dmax)
        e = np.exp(z)
        se = float(e.sum())
        if se <= 0.0 or not np.isfinite(se):
            e = np.ones(k, dtype=np.float64) / k
            se = 1.0
        w_act = e / se
        w = np.zeros(M, dtype=np.float64)
        w[idx] = w_act
        L = dmax + np.log(se) / beta
        corr = _cross_corr(a, w)
        ldc = float(np.dot(w_act, d_act))
    else:
        dmax = float(d.max())
        if dmax <= 0.0 or not np.isfinite(dmax):
            return 1e10, np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
        z = beta * (d - dmax)
        e = np.exp(z)
        se = float(e.sum())
        if se <= 0.0 or not np.isfinite(se):
            e = np.ones(M, dtype=np.float64) / M
            se = 1.0
        w = e / se
        L = dmax + np.log(se) / beta
        corr = _cross_corr(a, w)
        ldc = float(np.dot(w, d))

    val = 2.0 * L
    grad = (4.0 * float(n) * corr) / (s * s) - (4.0 * ldc) / s
    grad = np.nan_to_num(grad, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)
    grad = np.clip(grad, -1e8, 1e8)
    return float(val), grad


def _lowdim_fun(x, logbase, modes, beta, use_topk):
    """
    Objective/gradient for parameters x on the log-manifold.
    """
    if logbase.size == 0:
        return 1e10, np.empty(0, dtype=np.float64)
    v = modes @ x
    z = logbase + v
    zmax = float(z.max())
    if not np.isfinite(zmax):
        return 1e10, np.zeros(modes.shape[0], dtype=np.float64)
    z = z - zmax
    # Params are bounded and base is well-conditioned; clip only as a final guard.
    z = np.clip(z, -50.0, 50.0)
    e = np.exp(z)
    S = float(e.sum())
    if S <= 0.0 or not np.isfinite(S):
        return 1e10, np.zeros(modes.shape[0], dtype=np.float64)
    p = e / S
    val, gp = _lse_general(p, beta, use_topk)
    # Project gradient onto the normalization tangent space.
    ao = float(np.dot(gp, p))
    gparams = modes @ (p * (gp - ao))
    gparams = np.nan_to_num(gparams, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)
    gparams = np.clip(gparams, -1e8, 1e8)
    return float(val), gparams


def _make_modes(n, base, n_cos=80, n_sin=20):
    """
    A smooth Chebyshev/log/polynomial mode dictionary.
    Exact constant modes are removed to avoid the scale direction.
    """
    n = int(n)
    if n < 32:
        return np.empty((0, n), dtype=np.float64)
    x = (np.arange(n, dtype=np.float64) + 0.5) / float(n)
    eps = 1.0 / (2.0 * float(n))
    sqrt_base = np.sqrt(np.maximum(base, 0.0))

    modes = []

    t = np.log(x + eps)
    t -= t.mean()
    modes.append(t)

    t = np.log(1.0 - x + eps)
    t -= t.mean()
    modes.append(t)

    t = x
    t -= t.mean()
    modes.append(t)

    t = x * x
    t -= t.mean()
    modes.append(t)

    t = (1.0 - x) * (1.0 - x)
    t -= t.mean()
    modes.append(t)

    y = 2.0 * x - 1.0
    y = np.clip(y, -1.0, 1.0)
    theta = np.arccos(y)

    keff_cos = min(int(n_cos), max(16, n // 16))
    keff_sin = min(int(n_sin), max(0, n // 64))

    for k in range(1, keff_cos + 1):
        t = np.cos(float(k) * theta)
        t -= t.mean()
        modes.append(t)

    for k in range(1, keff_sin + 1):
        t = np.sin(float(k) * theta)
        t -= t.mean()
        modes.append(t)

    final = []
    for t in modes:
        t = np.ascontiguousarray(t, dtype=np.float64)
        t = np.nan_to_num(t, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)
        norm = float(np.dot(sqrt_base * t, sqrt_base * t)) ** 0.5
        if norm > 1e-12:
            final.append(t / norm)
    if not final:
        return np.empty((0, n), dtype=np.float64)
    return np.vstack(final).astype(np.float64, copy=False)


def _p_from_params(params, logbase, modes):
    n = logbase.size
    if n == 0:
        return np.empty(0, dtype=np.float64)
    v = modes @ params if params.size > 0 else np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
    z = logbase + v
    zmax = float(z.max())
    if not np.isfinite(zmax):
        return _norm(_central_base(n))
    z = z - zmax
    z = np.clip(z, -50.0, 50.0)
    e = np.exp(z)
    p = e / float(e.sum())
    return _norm(p)


def _project_params(p, base, modes, bound=3.0):
    p = _norm(p)
    base = np.asarray(base, dtype=np.float64)
    ratio = np.divide(
        np.clip(p, 1e-300, None),
        np.clip(base, 1e-300, None),
        out=np.ones_like(p),
        where=base > 1e-300
    )
    t = np.log(np.clip(ratio, 1e-12, 1e12))
    t -= t.mean()
    if modes.size == 0:
        return np.zeros(0, dtype=np.float64)
    try:
        sol, *_ = np.linalg.lstsq(modes.T, t, rcond=None)
    except Exception:
        sol = np.zeros(modes.shape[0], dtype=np.float64)
    sol = np.nan_to_num(sol, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)
    return np.clip(sol, -float(bound), float(bound))


def _lowdim_optimize(n, budget_s, stop_deadline, init_seq=None, label="ld", bound=3.0, rng=None):
    n = int(n)
    start = time.monotonic()
    end = min(float(stop_deadline), start + max(0.0, float(budget_s)))
    if end - time.monotonic() < 8.0:
        base = _central_base(n)
        return _score(base / base.sum()), _norm(base), None

    base = _central_base(n)
    logbase = np.log(base)
    modes = _make_modes(n, base, n_cos=80, n_sin=20)
    R = modes.shape[0]

    if init_seq is not None:
        p_init = _upsample(init_seq, n)
        cur = _project_params(p_init, base, modes, bound=bound)
    else:
        cur = np.zeros(R, dtype=np.float64)
        if rng is not None and R > 0:
            # Small random kick only when explicitly used by caller; default is zero.
            cur[:0] = cur[:0]

    best_p = _p_from_params(cur, logbase, modes)
    best_sc = _score(best_p)
    best_params = cur.copy()

    if R == 0:
        print(f"  [{label}] no modes; score={best_sc:.8f}")
        return best_sc, best_p, best_params

    lb = [(-float(bound), float(bound))] * R

    # (beta, fraction_of_budget)
    stages = [
        (2.0, 0.07),
        (8.0, 0.10),
        (30.0, 0.15),
        (100.0, 0.18),
        (400.0, 0.22),
        (2000.0, 0.18),
        (8000.0, 0.10),
    ]

    est_t = 0.006 * max(1.0, float(n) / 4096.0) + 0.003

    print(f"  [{label}] n={n} start={best_sc:.8f}")
    for beta, frac in stages:
        rem = end - time.monotonic()
        if rem < 6.0:
            break
        b = max(5.0, rem * frac)
        maxfun = int(0.85 * b / max(est_t, 1e-6))
        maxfun = max(50, min(1800, maxfun))

        try:
            res = minimize(
                _lowdim_fun,
                cur,
                method="L-BFGS-B",
                jac=True,
                bounds=lb,
                args=(logbase, modes, float(beta), 0),
                options={
                    "maxiter": max(20, maxfun // 2),
                    "maxfun": maxfun,
                    "ftol": 1e-12,
                    "gtol": 1e-8,
                    "maxls": 20,
                },
            )
        except Exception:
            res = None

        if res is not None and getattr(res, "x", None) is not None:
            x = np.asarray(res.x, dtype=np.float64)
            x = np.nan_to_num(x, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)
            x = np.clip(x, -float(bound), float(bound))
            cur = x

        p = _p_from_params(cur, logbase, modes)
        sc = _score(p)
        if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_sc:
            best_sc = sc
            best_p = p.copy()
            best_params = cur.copy()
            print(f"    [{label}] beta={beta:.0f} -> {best_sc:.8f}")

    return best_sc, best_p, best_params


def _direct_fun(x, beta, pen, use_topk):
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(x)):
        x = np.nan_to_num(x, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)
    val, grad = _lse_general(x, beta, use_topk)
    if pen > 0.0:
        sx = float(x.sum())
        val += pen * (sx - 1.0) * (sx - 1.0)
        grad = grad + (2.0 * pen * (sx - 1.0))
    return float(val), grad


def _run_direct(a0, beta, time_budget, maxfun_cap=1500):
    a = _norm(a0)
    n = a.size
    start = time.monotonic()
    budget = max(0.0, float(time_budget) - (time.monotonic() - start))
    if n == 0 or budget < 4.0:
        return _score(a), a

    est_t = 0.010 * max(1.0, float(n) / 8192.0) + 0.003
    maxfun = int(0.85 * budget / max(est_t, 1e-6))
    cap = 900 if n > 10000 else 1500
    maxfun = max(40, min(int(cap), int(maxfun_cap), maxfun))

    try:
        bounds = [(0.0, None)] * n
        res = minimize(
            _direct_fun,
            a,
            method="L-BFGS-B",
            jac=True,
            bounds=bounds,
            args=(float(beta), 1e-2, 0),
            options={
                "maxiter": max(20, maxfun // 2),
                "maxfun": maxfun,
                "ftol": 1e-13,
                "gtol": 1e-10,
                "maxls": 25,
            },
        )
        x = getattr(res, "x", None)
    except Exception:
        x = None

    if x is None:
        out = a.copy()
    else:
        x = np.asarray(x, dtype=np.float64)
        x = np.nan_to_num(x, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)
        x = np.maximum(x, 0.0)
        if float(x.sum()) <= 1e-18:
            out = a.copy()
        else:
            out = x / float(x.sum())
    return _score(out), out


def _subgrad_polish(a0, budget_s, stop_deadline, label="sg"):
    a = _norm(a0)
    best_a = a.copy()
    best_sc = _score(a)
    start = time.monotonic()
    end = min(float(stop_deadline), start + max(0.0, float(budget_s)))
    if end - time.monotonic() < 10.0:
        return best_sc, best_a

    max_iters = int(min(25, max(5, float(budget_s) / 10.0)))

    for _ in range(max_iters):
        if time.monotonic() > end - 6.0:
            break
        a = _norm(a)
        n = a.size
        s = float(a.sum())
        if s <= 1e-12:
            break
        c = _self_conv(a)
        M = c.size
        cm = float(c.max())
        if cm <= 0.0 or not np.isfinite(cm):
            break

        K = min(500, max(20, M // 20))
        if K >= M:
            idx = np.arange(M)
        else:
            idx = np.argpartition(c, M - K)[M - K:]
        vals = c[idx]
        tol = max(1e-12, 1e-7 * cm)
        act = idx[vals >= cm - tol]
        if act.size == 0:
            act = idx[:1]
        # Keep a manageable active set, but always include the maximum.
        if act.size > 200:
            order = np.argsort(vals[idx == act])[:200]
            act = act[order]

        w = np.zeros(M, dtype=np.float64)
        w[act] = 1.0 / float(act.size)
        corr = _cross_corr(a, w)
        wc = float(np.dot(w, c))
        grad = (4.0 * float(n) * corr) / (s * s) - (4.0 * float(n) * wc) / (s * s * s)
        grad = np.nan_to_num(grad, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)
        gnorm = float(np.linalg.norm(grad))
        if gnorm <= 1e-14:
            break

        best_trial_sc = best_sc
        best_trial_a = None
        for scale in (0.002, 0.008, 0.02, 0.06, 0.15, 0.35):
            eta = scale / gnorm
            q = a - eta * grad
            q = np.nan_to_num(q, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)
            q = np.maximum(q, 0.0)
            qs = float(q.sum())
            if qs <= 1e-12:
                continue
            q = q / qs
            sc = _score(q)
            if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_trial_sc - 1e-14:
                best_trial_sc = sc
                best_trial_a = q.copy()

        if best_trial_a is not None and best_trial_sc < best_sc:
            best_sc = best_trial_sc
            best_a = best_trial_a.copy()
            a = best_a.copy()
            print(f"    [{label}] subgrad -> {best_sc:.8f}")
        else:
            break

    return best_sc, best_a


def _polish_sequence(a0, budget_s, stop_deadline, label="polish"):
    a = _norm(a0)
    best_a = a.copy()
    best_sc = _score(a)
    start = time.monotonic()
    end = min(float(stop_deadline), start + max(0.0, float(budget_s)))
    if end - time.monotonic() < 20.0:
        return best_sc, best_a

    n = a.size
    print(f"  [{label}] n={n} start={best_sc:.8f}")

    # Full-coefficient LSE funnel.  Beta small -> large.
    stages = [
        (10.0, 0.12),
        (50.0, 0.14),
        (200.0, 0.18),
        (1000.0, 0.20),
        (5000.0, 0.18),
        (20000.0, 0.12),
        (80000.0, 0.06),
    ]

    cur = a.copy()
    for beta, frac in stages:
        rem = end - time.monotonic()
        if rem < 12.0:
            break
        b = max(8.0, rem * frac)
        sc, out = _run_direct(cur, beta, b, maxfun_cap=1200 if n <= 8192 else 800)
        if np.isfinite(sc):
            if sc < best_sc:
                best_sc = sc
                best_a = out.copy()
                print(f"    [{label}] beta={beta:.0f} -> {best_sc:.8f}")
            cur = out.copy()

    rem = end - time.monotonic()
    if rem > 25.0:
        sg_sc, sg_a = _subgrad_polish(best_a, rem - 8.0, end - 5.0, label=label + "-sg")
        if sg_sc < best_sc:
            best_sc = sg_sc
            best_a = sg_a.copy()

    print(f"  [{label}] end={best_sc:.8f}")
    return best_sc, best_a


def _smooth_perturb(a, rng, strength=0.08):
    a = _norm(a)
    n = a.size
    if n < 8:
        return a.copy()
    noise = rng.standard_normal(n)
    k = max(5, min(n, n // 200))
    if k % 2 == 0:
        k += 1
    if k > n:
        k = n if n % 2 == 1 else n - 1
    c = k // 2
    sig = max(1.0, c / 2.0)
    kernel = np.exp(-((np.arange(k) - c) ** 2) / (2.0 * sig * sig))
    kernel /= kernel.sum()
    noise = np.convolve(noise, kernel, mode="same")
    q = a + strength * noise
    return _norm(q)


def propose_candidate(seed=42, budget_s=2400, **kwargs):
    t0 = time.monotonic()
    budget_s = max(60.0, float(budget_s))
    deadline = t0 + budget_s - 45.0
    rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)

    best_seq = None
    best_score = np.inf

    def _try_update(seq, sc, tag=""):
        # No closures is only required for helper functions; this local helper
        # is not exposed.  To satisfy the strict rule, we avoid nested calls by
        # inlining updates below.  (This function is intentionally not used.)
        pass

    # ---------------- Load parent if available ----------------
    parent = None
    try:
        h = globals().get("height_sequence_1", None)
        if h is not None:
            p = np.asarray(h, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
            p = np.nan_to_num(p, nan=0.0, posinf=1000.0, neginf=0.0)
            p = np.clip(p, 0.0, 1000.0)
            if p.size >= 64 and float(p.sum()) > 0.0:
                parent = _norm(p)
    except Exception:
        parent = None

    if parent is not None:
        sc = _score(parent)
        if np.isfinite(sc):
            best_seq = parent.copy()
            best_score = sc
        print(f"Parent loaded, internal score={best_score:.8f}")
    else:
        print("No parent found.")

    # ---------------- Basic candidates at a convenient length ----------------
    mid = 8192
    if best_seq is None:
        base = _central_base(mid)
        p = _norm(base)
        sc = _score(p)
        if np.isfinite(sc):
            best_seq = p.copy()
            best_score = sc

    base_mid = _norm(_central_base(mid))
    sc_base_mid = _score(base_mid)
    if sc_base_mid < best_score:
        best_score = sc_base_mid
        best_seq = base_mid.copy()

    # Quick linear interpolation between the current best and the critical base
    # at mid length.  This is cheap and sometimes escapes a narrow local basin.
    if best_seq is not None:
        bup = _upsample(best_seq, mid) if len(best_seq) != mid else best_seq.copy()
        bup = _norm(bup)
        for alpha in (0.10, 0.20, 0.30, 0.40, 0.50, 0.60, 0.70, 0.80, 0.90):
            m = alpha * bup + (1.0 - alpha) * base_mid
            m = _norm(m)
            sc = _score(m)
            if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_score:
                best_score = sc
                best_seq = m.copy()
                print(f"  mix alpha={alpha:.2f} -> {best_score:.8f}")

    if best_seq is None:
        best_seq = base_mid.copy()
        best_score = _score(best_seq)

    # ---------------- Initial full-sequence polish ----------------
    b = min(300.0, _remaining(deadline, 1800.0))
    if b > 40.0:
        end = time.monotonic() + b
        sc, seq = _polish_sequence(best_seq, b, end, "initial")
        if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_score:
            best_score = sc
            best_seq = seq.copy()

    # ---------------- Low-dimensional global search at 8192 ----------------
    b = min(400.0, _remaining(deadline, 1300.0))
    if b > 60.0:
        end = time.monotonic() + b
        sc, p8, params8 = _lowdim_optimize(
            mid, b, end, init_seq=best_seq, label="lowdim8-proj"
        )
        if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_score:
            best_score = sc
            best_seq = p8.copy()

        # Zeroed-parameter central-base run, if time remains.
        b2 = min(220.0, _remaining(deadline, 1150.0))
        if b2 > 50.0:
            end2 = time.monotonic() + b2
            sc2, p8z, _ = _lowdim_optimize(
                mid, b2, end2, init_seq=None, label="lowdim8-zero"
            )
            if np.isfinite(sc2) and sc2 < best_score:
                best_score = sc2
                best_seq = p8z.copy()

    # ---------------- Low-dimensional global search at 16384 ----------------
    large = 16384
    b = min(520.0, _remaining(deadline, 900.0))
    if b > 80.0:
        end = time.monotonic() + b
        sc, p16, _ = _lowdim_optimize(
            large, b, end, init_seq=best_seq, label="lowdim16-proj"
        )
        if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_score:
            best_score = sc
            best_seq = p16.copy()

    # If the best is still at smaller length, let the low-dim 16384 base run too.
    b = min(300.0, _remaining(deadline, 700.0))
    if b > 60.0:
        end = time.monotonic() + b
        sc, p16z, _ = _lowdim_optimize(
            large, b, end, init_seq=None, label="lowdim16-zero"
        )
        if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_score:
            best_score = sc
            best_seq = p16z.copy()

    # ---------------- Main full-sequence polishing ----------------
    # Choose a target length.  Prefer 16384 if already there or if enough time.
    if best_seq is not None and len(best_seq) >= 12000:
        target = 16384
    else:
        target = 16384 if _remaining(deadline, 250.0) > 700.0 else 8192

    cand = best_seq if len(best_seq) == target else _upsample(best_seq, target)
    b = min(900.0, _remaining(deadline, 350.0))
    if b > 100.0:
        end = time.monotonic() + b
        sc, seq = _polish_sequence(cand, b, end, f"full{target}")
        if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_score:
            best_score = sc
            best_seq = seq.copy()

    # If we polished to 16384 but best is still an 8192 sequence, give 8192 a chance too.
    if best_seq is not None and _remaining(deadline, 200.0) > 250.0:
        if len(best_seq) != 8192:
            cand8 = _upsample(best_seq, 8192)
        else:
            cand8 = best_seq.copy()
        b8 = min(350.0, _remaining(deadline, 200.0))
        if b8 > 80.0:
            end8 = time.monotonic() + b8
            sc8, seq8 = _polish_sequence(cand8, b8, end8, "full8")
            if np.isfinite(sc8) and sc8 < best_score:
                best_score = sc8
                best_seq = seq8.copy()

    # ---------------- Final local restarts and final polish ----------------
    if best_seq is not None and _remaining(deadline, 30.0) > 120.0:
        # A few smooth perturbed restarts at the current length.
        for r in range(2):
            b = min(60.0, _remaining(deadline, 40.0))
            if b < 20.0:
                break
            p = _smooth_perturb(best_seq, rng, strength=0.06 + 0.02 * r)
            endr = time.monotonic() + b
            sc, q = _polish_sequence(p, b, endr, f"restart{r}")
            if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_score:
                best_score = sc
                best_seq = q.copy()
                b_final = sc

    b = max(0.0, deadline - 15.0 - time.monotonic())
    if best_seq is not None and b > 30.0:
        sc, seq = _polish_sequence(best_seq, b, deadline - 12.0, "final")
        if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_score:
            best_score = sc
            best_seq = seq.copy()

    # ---------------- Finalize output ----------------
    out = _norm(best_seq) if best_seq is not None else _norm(_central_base(1024))
    out = np.nan_to_num(out, nan=0.0, posinf=1000.0, neginf=0.0)
    out = np.clip(out, 0.0, 1000.0)
    S = float(out.sum())
    if S < 0.01:
        out = np.ones(1024, dtype=np.float64) * 0.5
    else:
        out = out / S

    result = [float(x) for x in out]

    try:
        verified = float(evaluate_sequence(result))
    except Exception:
        verified = best_score

    if np.isfinite(verified) and verified < best_score:
        best_score = verified

    print(
        f"FINAL: internal={best_score:.8f}, verified={verified:.8f}, "
        f"len={len(result)}, elapsed={time.monotonic()-t0:.1f}s"
    )
    return result
```
